<div class="col-sm-6 pad-left">
<h3 class="p1"><span class="s1">Good content grabs your attention. Great content inspires action.</span></h3>
<p class="p1"><span class="s1">So how do you harness the power of authentic, meaningful content, in which consumers will engage? Influencer marketing has proven to be the answer. Influencer Marketing is the most effective way to reengage and motivate consumers because it harnesses the power of authentic, meaningful content, created by consumers, for consumers. It’s trusted and valuable, rather than interruptive and contrived.</span></p>
<p class="p1" style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/product/"><span class="s1">SCALE YOUR INFLUENCER MARKETING<span class="Apple-converted-space">  </span>&gt;</span></a></p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="row no-gutter swap-mobile">
<div class="col-sm-6 pad-right divider-top">
<h3 class="p1"><span class="s1">Longtail content, maximized by influence, optimized for engagement.</span></h3>
<p class="p1"><span class="s1"> Influencer marketing goes past reach and clicks to continuous engagement and conversations that drive commerce, giving you metrics that matter and align with your business goals, such as: attracting new customers, increasing repeat purchases, driving customer loyalty, maximizing customer lifetime revenue. Influencer Marketing resonates with your audience and it lives on beyond traditional ad spend, thwarts adblock and is designed for longtail impact.</span></p>
<p class="p1" style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/tp_resource/nielsen-case-study/"><span class="s1">CHECK OUT THE CASE STUDY<span class="Apple-converted-space">  </span>&gt;</span></a></p>

<div class="col-sm-6 divider-top-mobile">
<h3>Content should be a dynamic and appreciating asset.</h3>
<p>Good content is the backbone of great marketing. Influencer-Generated Content is lower cost and higher impact with a built-in distribution engine. Create more engaging videos, blogs, podcasts, social content, images, and other content created by influencers at scale. More engaging content translates into direct ROI.</p>
<p class="p1" style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/the-ultimate-influencer-marketing-guide/"><span class="s1">LEARN MORE ABOUT INFLUENCER MARKETING<span class="Apple-converted-space">  </span>&gt;</span></a></p>

<div class="small-container">
<h2>Influencer marketing. Powered by data, designed for convenience.</h2>
<div class="process-categories">
<div class="process active" data-process="Discover"><div class="icon"><img class="lazyload" data-src="https://www.tapinfluence.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/12/discovery_icon.png" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw=="/></div><span>Discover</span></div><div class="process" data-process="Workflow"><div class="icon"><img class="lazyload" data-src="https://www.tapinfluence.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/12/workflow_icon.png" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw=="/></div><span>Workflow</span></div><div class="process" data-process="Measure"><div class="icon"><img class="lazyload" data-src="https://www.tapinfluence.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/12/measure_icon.png" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw=="/></div><span>Measure</span></div><div class="process" data-process="Amplify"><div class="icon"><img class="lazyload" data-src="https://www.tapinfluence.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/12/amplify_icon.png" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw=="/></div><span>Amplify</span></div> </div>
<div class="process-descriptions">
<div class="process-video active" data-process="Discover"><p>How do you find the right influencers? That’s the number one challenge marketers identify with influencer marketing. But it’s not just finding influencers, it’s finding the right influencers who also want to work for your brand. Influencer discovery gives you a 360 degree perspective on the influencer so you’re guaranteed the best match possible with your brand. It’s also really easy, you get results within seconds.</p>
<p style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/product/">Learn More &gt;</a></p>
</div><div class="process-video" data-process="Workflow"><p>What are the best practices for working with influencers? How do you work with influencers? How much do you pay influencers? All very good questions. Workflow was built with convenience in mind so you know exactly how to run an influencer marketing program from creative briefing, content creation, promotion, and how much to pay.</p>
<p style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/product/">Learn More &gt;</a></p>
</div><div class="process-video" data-process="Measure"><p>What are the right influencer marketing KPIs? How do I measure success across multiple platforms? What are the right influencer marketing benchmarks? We got your back and measure everything including reach, views, media value, conversion and ROI. We also have cross platform and industry benchmarks. We’re marketers and we know how hard it is to prove marketing investment.</p>
<p style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/product/">Learn More &gt;</a></p>
</div><div class="process-video" data-process="Amplify"><p>Creating content, finding the right influencers and measuring success are only one part of the formula. Success can’t be had if your content isn’t getting out in front of the right people at the right time. TapInfluence has a built in cross platform content distribution tool optimized for performance.</p>
<p style="text-align: right;"><a href="https://www.tapinfluence.com/product/">Learn More &gt;</a></p>
